# Software standards report

This is a raw Software Standards Bootstrap v0.1.1 proposal for Hoop at baseline `3e0091b89fdaa3912f4f8e7b33fbb3104e47d71e`. It is an experiment artifact, not an adopted Hoop policy.

## Coverage

Inventory coverage was complete: 2,285 candidate files and 21,798,146 candidate bytes were scanned without truncation. The inventory indexed 2,239 files and 13,968,340 bytes. Exclusions are preserved verbatim in the manifest; notably, secret-like and generated content was not used as semantic evidence.

The release-installed `software-standards-bootstrap` skill was evaluator input. It was absent from the baseline inventory and is not an accepted artifact in this proposal.

## Accepted output summary

- Five semantic rules make Hoop-specific architecture, protocol, analytics, and deployment obligations available to agents through the generated `AGENTS.md` projection.
- Three verification recipes point to existing repository commands for OSS Go tests, React lint/build checks, and OpenAPI regeneration.
- Two Agent Skills capture decision-bearing feature-flag and gateway-route workflows.
- One automation proposal describes a possible future check for analytics drift. It is not implemented or active and is omitted from `AGENTS.md`.

## Limitations

- No Hoop build, test, lint, formatter, hook, package-manager, migration, or generated-code command was executed during bootstrap.
- The proposal reflects one public commit and the repository-maintained evidence available in its complete SSB inventory. It does not represent Hoop review, approval, adoption, or current Qodo enforcement.
- Existing `.claude/` configuration and skills were treated as repository evidence. The baseline had no `AGENTS.md`, `.agents/`, or `.software-standards/` pack.
- Verification recipes record commands and expected success conditions; they do not claim those commands passed.
